# Damage and control spells — proposed video game rules

**Pending exact authored review.** These25 spell identities preserve their original class/school/rank access. NEW choices are explicit and apply only to their named source. Use the full shared procedures and the spell together. This is an authored candidate, not implemented or balance-certified gameplay.

## Shared operating rules

### scope

All additions below are NEW scoped next25 proposals requiring actual GPT conference. They do not alter the source records, existing accepted spells or the global geometry/interrupt engine. Original exact records and all actual class access are embedded per spell. Current02 restores all18 earlier full authored procedures and36 original cases. Source-specific restored procedure clauses control over generic current01 defaults. Earlier proposal NEW labels remain pending.

### casting

Use the chosen actual class/school/rank access, minimum class level and independently acquired repertoire in completeSourceAndAccess. Base RP remains the exact class row (r4 7, r5 10, r6 13, r7 17, r8 21, r9 26). All actual additional costs, Dissonance, Refrain, components, normal concentration transitions and cast-begin hooks commit before interruption. No generic upcast, new Action, optional class exception or permission follows from having RP. A later counter, immunity, blocked payload or failed save refunds nothing. Components remain actual Core V/S/F except Circle of Death retains V/S/M-or-F; its M alternative needs its source item, and no invented component cost is added. Mental Bomb is a Reaction; other24 casts are Actions. The reaction trigger is explicitly NEW below, not an inherited Core fact.

READY INHERITANCE CLARIFICATION (accepted interface, not a new interrupt): for an ordinary direct cast, choose the card's required targets/point/geometry at its normal commitment checkpoint and revalidate after actual cast interruptions. For a legally readied Action-time spell, the accepted Ready rule instead chooses spell/mode and pays/components/Dissonance/Refrain/cast hooks at preparation; target/area point and the target-dependent orientation/recipient selection occur and are revalidated at release. A locked non-target mode such as Meteor's damage identity, Destructive Wave's damage type, or Elemental Devastation's chosen element is not changed after preparation. The ordinary Reaction release and held concentration remain required. There is no second cast, counter window or payment at release, and effect duration begins there. Mental Bomb is a Reaction-time spell and cannot be prepared by generic Ready. These distinctions do not add an information/presentation permission or a sixth Fate/RNG whitelist entry.

### digitalGeometry

NEW scoped geometry for these25 cards: use actual world-space 3D occupied body volumes and Euclidean feet, not display sprites or the historical grid diagonal metric. Single-target range is the nearest actual caster-to-target body distance. A chosen area center must be a perceived point within the stated range from the actual caster body, with originating line of effect. A creature qualifies only if at least one actual body point intersects the closed specified volume and has line of effect from the relevant area origin/center. Tangency counts as intersection. Total cover prevents that application. Fix the whole recipient set and geometry after cast interruptions and before damage; destroying cover gives no second scan. Friendly targets and the caster are included whenever an ALL-creature shape actually includes them; ENEMIES means creatures the caster already ordinarily identifies as enemies at this snapshot, with no hidden-hostility or faction scan. No spell title creates an object-targeting permission. The earlier source-required individual perception predicate for enemy-only selection is retained; awareness without the required actual perception does not silently add a target. A card's own explicit origin exclusion, object mode, prism/cone shape or actual typed predicate takes precedence.

### lineAndCone

NEW scoped digital presentation of each actual proposed shape, not a global template: Meteor Spear retains its60x5x5 rectangular prism; Sunbeam retains60ft length/5ft-diameter cylinder; Supernova Lance proposes100ft length/5ft-diameter cylinder. Cone of Cold retains60ft axial length with full diameter d at axial distance d, so radius=d/2 and far diameter60. Use exact continuous Euclidean body intersection, not a tabletop raster convention. Horrid Wilting proposes an oriented30ft cube with chosen center in30ft range. Cast-specific origins and line-of-effect remain actual. None changes the geometry of an accepted other spell.

NEW DC25-SHAPE-POSE-01, limited to these proposed cards: store the actual shape origin/center and its complete orientation at the legal geometry-selection checkpoint (normal commitment, or target selection at a legal Ready release). For Meteor Spear record a unit forward axis and two mutually perpendicular unit cross-section axes forming an orthonormal frame; points are origin+s*forward+u*right+v*up, 0<=s<=60 and |u|,|v|<=2.5. This removes undefined roll about a vertical/oblique spear axis. For Horrid Wilting record three orthonormal cube axes; each signed coordinate relative to the perceived chosen center lies in[-15,15]. No rounding of a sphere or cone to occupied grid tiles is permitted. Cylinders and the circular cone are rotationally symmetric about their stored axes; do not add an arbitrary prism height to them. Closed-body tangency counts except the card's explicit origin-only caster contact exclusion. Range/line-of-effect/field-origin predicates continue to use their actual source, not a rendering transform. Geometry choices are declared before results and never selected to fit a later save, hidden-defense result or damage outcome. This does not change another accepted spell or the global movement geometry.

### saveAndDamage

A specified save uses8+PB+the real casting modifier under the actual placement/class and actual modifiers. Save outcomes are binary, not degrees; save damage never criticals. For each card use exactly its success branch, not a universal half-damage default. Unless a card expressly says per-target, use one shared raw pool for its area/chain and separate recipient damage packets, roots and actual defenses. Elemental Devastation explicitly rolls separately per enemy. Mixed typed parts of one actual hit stay one damage event for Wound/zero/lifecycle consequences; Meteor Spear alone uses its explicitly NEW DC25-MIXED-DEFENSE-02 apportionment at the one actually authorized source stage; this is not a general mixed-damage engine. Halve each specified raw typed part with floor only when that card says half, before applicable defenses. Apply ordinary real damage, temporary HP, Wounds, zero/death/Stable/Dying and source-specific callbacks once. No creature type supplies an unprinted blanket mental or damage immunity.

### rootsAndSuppression

At the actual permitted damage application create each recipient root under the accepted creation-time dealer/Null rules, even when the RNG pool is shared. Caster is the dealer for these direct spells; an arc origin is not a new dealer. Separate recipients and genuinely separate casts keep distinct roots. Apply current Lesser Antimagic, Globe and genuine defenses to actual payload locus/origin/rank. Globe blocks only its covered incoming rank0–5 application; rank6+ is outside that ceiling. An initially prevented rider does not establish or wait for later suppression ending. Established condition records retain identity, clocks and spent history through suppression; expired/ended records never replay. Completed instantaneous damage is not dispellable after the fact. No attack, roll, concentration, resource or root is manufactured by presentation or reentry.

### control

Stunned and Paralyzed attempt independent hard-denial components under the accepted one-source-character/target/encounter guard; a prior spent guard prevents only that new hard component with no unprinted fallback. A failed save can still resolve independent damage. Damage-type immunity does not prevent a separately legal condition, and condition immunity does not prevent damage. Establishment spends the hard guard once; no later recast, source rename, suppression or expiration refunds it. Prone, Blinded, Frightened and the explicit limited Reaction/movement losses use their actual narrower scope; they do not acquire hard-denial immunity by association.

### duration

Use each complete spell-specific endpoint, including its named regular-turn or actual attached-phase bridge. A generic default does not replace those original authored records. Preserve original typed/absolute clocks through reorder/absence/extra turns; initial prevention creates no deferred component. Any new companion/no-schedule bridge remains explicitly proposed and requires review. For the seven newly authored high-rank cards with no earlier record, the current01 one-target-round fallback is retained only where actually specified: original next future regular END or6sec outside/no independent schedule, without inventing creature turns. Prone follows its actual stand/cure ending.

### recordsAndCleanup

Record exact generating spell ID, school/rank, class/source owner, cast/event order, target, components, saved outcome, source DC, initial/root IDs, actual absolute or typed deadline, concentration link, spent guard and suppression state. Different paid casts keep independent records and do not add condition magnitude; deleting/ending one never removes another. A successfully established same-owner named condition can retain independent source records here; it cannot bypass a hard guard or trigger duplicate ticks. Death/ended identity, actual removal, duration or concentration ends only the covered application. Destroyed/ended creature identities are not silently recreated. Damage types and names add no Burning/Frozen, ice shell, soul, plane, alignment, faction or automatic environmental effect absent that card’s actual printed promise.

## Complete spell procedures

## Orb of Annihilation — orb-of-annihilation

| Actual class | School | Rank | Minimum class level | Base RP |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Crimson Agent | Evocation | 4 | 11 | 7 |
| Dragoon | Evocation | 4 | 18 | 7 |
| Mage | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |
| Wizard | Evocation | 4 | 7 | 7 |

### completeText

Action; range 60 feet; V/S/F; instantaneous, no concentration. KEEP exactly one target, 8d8 force and DEX half. NEW eligibility: one perceived creature or independently legal object with line of effect. A direct object must be unattended, held by you or deliberately offered by a willing holder; this grants no hostile worn/carried inventory targeting. Choose the actual object body, not an arbitrary structural collection. Resolve creature DEX normally. NEW object bridge: if an actual object profile explicitly supplies its own DEX save, use it; otherwise an ordinary inanimate object cannot evade and fails this save without inventing an ability score, proficiency or a bearer’s substitute save. Use actual object durability and material defenses; absent a needed object profile, resolve its legitimate statistics before using it as a mechanical target rather than assigning free destruction. A legally committed cast whose target is no longer eligible at resolution fizzles with no retarget, splash or last-location impact. Roll 8d8 once: failure takes full force, success half floored, then actual defenses. This is a saving-throw damage event, not a spell attack. The title supplies no disintegration, unhealable HP loss, automatic killing, object erasure, antimagic, persistent orb, exceptional artifact permission or special zero-HP threshold. Apply the ordinary creature/object zero-HP outcome once. Destruction of cover or a recipient does not pass damage through to a second target. Separate real paid casts are separate instantaneous events, with no stored orb or ongoing rider.

## Voltaic Detonation — voltaic-detonation

### completeText

Action; range 60 feet; V/S/F; instantaneous detonation with its printed one-turn Stunned rider, no concentration. NEW choose one perceived point with line of effect and detonate a ten-foot-radius sphere there. Affect all eligible creatures including allies/caster in the actual origin line of effect; no object packet or conductive chain. Snapshot all initial recipients before consequences. Each makes DEX against the cast DC; one shared 6d8 lightning roll deals full on failure or half on success. After that recipient’s damage, a still-continuing creature independently makes CON against the same recorded DC to resist Stunned. The DEX outcome, positive damage and lightning defenses do not substitute for this CON result. Actual Stunned/control immunity or already spent source-character/target/encounter hard-denial allowance blocks that component only; it grants no fallback, banked stun or later automatic attempt. On failed CON, if the hard component actually establishes, spend that shared allowance and apply Core Stunned, including its ordinary concentration loss. KEEP one hostile turn: its base endpoint is the end of the target’s first future regular turn, excluding the entire current turn if underway; outside initiative use application +6 seconds. Keep the original real/virtual endpoint through extra turns, reordering and absence. NEW an owner-attached target uses the end of its next future actual regular attached phase, excluding a phase underway; with no such phase retain a six-second absolute endpoint and create no actions/turn. There is no repeated ending save. Initial prevention gives no record; later suppression leaves the spent guard and original deadline, so return before expiry may restore only the remaining established condition. Ending grants no refunded actions or restored concentration. An application that really kills or ends the creature before the CON step attaches no rider; merely reaching zero HP is not automatically death. A successfully committed instantaneous area resolves its snapshotted recipients even if its own earlier damage incapacitates the caster.

### completeText

Action; range60feet; V/S/F. KEEP one target, CON save,5d8 cold and failed-save Paralyzed for two rounds. NEW target one perceived creature with line of effect; objects are ineligible. One CON save governs both components. Success negates damage and Paralyzed; failure deals5d8 cold and, after damage, attempts actual Paralyzed on the same continuing eligible creature. Cold resistance/immunity and Paralyzed/control defenses are independent; a cold-immune failed-save target can be paralyzed unless an actual relevant defense prevents it. Admit the rider only if the existing combined source-character/target/encounter whole-Action denial allowance is available, and spend that allowance only on actual establishment. If the allowance is unavailable or an immunity prevents the rider, the failed-save cold damage still resolves with no invented soft replacement or refund. NEW no concentration; immediate damage is instantaneous and the separately recorded magical Paralyzed rider ends at the target’s second future original regular-turn END after application, excluding an entire already-underway target turn; outside initiative it ends after12elapsed seconds. No repeat save is added. Paralyzed uses the actual reviewed condition and its real incapacitation, movement, attack and concentration consequences; merely calling the creature frozen solid does not add Core Frozen, an ice object, a container, suffocation, weight change or preservation. Actual condition cure, qualifying source removal, expiry or death ends this source-owned rider. Suppression suspends its consequences for the suppressed interval while its original endpoint runs; ending suppression never restores concentration already lost or refunds spent hard-control history. Same-source repeated hard control cannot refresh this rider through the spent allowance; a distinct eligible owner retains its own allowance and duration. No damage ticks, conjured ice, shattering multiplier or generic fire-thaw method is granted.

## Meteor Spear — meteor-spear

| Actual class | School | Rank | Minimum class level | Base RP |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Crimson Agent | Evocation | 5 | 15 | 10 |
| Mage | Evocation | 5 | 9 | 10 |
| Wizard | Evocation | 5 | 9 | 10 |

### completeText

Action; range60feet; V/S/F; Instantaneous. KEEP a line of enemies and8d6 fire PLUS3d6 bludgeoning. NEW choose a straight direction from a real point on the caster’s occupied boundary; the finite rectangular prism is sixty feet long, five feet wide and five feet high, with its cross-section centered on the declared axis. Orient the axis legally in three dimensions; the origin alone does not include the caster. Affect all currently perceived creatures affirmatively designated hostile that intersect the prism with origin line of effect; no chosen target cap, hidden-allegiance test or selective post-roll omission. Allies not designated hostile are excluded by the source enemy clause. Each selected creature makes DEX. NEW failure takes both full typed amounts; success takes half of each separately, rounded down. Roll8d6 fire once and3d6 bludgeoning once for the whole line. Apply source/type-specific defenses and source-preserving damage sharing before a single simultaneous original-recipient damage event and its actual callbacks. The line stops applying through actual barriers/total cover and never bores a tunnel. Destruction by a genuinely separate effect follows its real timing; this cast does not recalculate additional targets exposed during its own event. Impale describes only these printed damage components: no pin, forced movement, Grappled, Bleed, automatic Wound, lingering embedded object or reusable ammunition. No objects, carried gear, building structure or ground become spell damage recipients. After resolving the initial snapshot there is no line hazard, later entry effect or concentration.

NEW DC25-MIXED-DEFENSE-02, proposed ONLY for Meteor Spear and an actually authorized descendant of that same damage packet. This allocates an already applicable aggregate flat reduction; it does not grant one, move its source stage, copy it onto every component, or rerun mitigation on a post-defense transfer where accepted sharing disallows that mitigation.

After the spell's separate fire/bludgeoning save rounding, select applicable defenses under their actual sources. For competing class flat reductions covered by CLASS_PLAYTEST_RULES use one strongest flat amount, not one before-resistance amount plus another after-resistance amount. Spinal Plating's explicit after-resistance wording changes the stage of that selected source, not the number of reductions granted. If an accepted source independently permits a separate stage, retain only that express permission. NEW Meteor-only equal-amount tie completion: when no more specific source chooses between equal competing flat reductions, choose the lexically first stable source-mechanical-ID and then application-ID, fixed before any resistance/immune outcome; this is not a player optimization or a global stacking rule. A larger ordinary class reduction therefore wins over a smaller Spinal Plating reduction; Spinal applies after resistance only when it is the selected applicable reduction. All real acquisition, active mutation, use and expiry prerequisites still apply.

At the selected aggregate stage, combine entries of the same canonical damage type. Let their nonnegative integer eligible amounts be a_i, A=sum(a_i), and B=min(applicable reduction,A). If A=0, allocate nothing. Otherwise assign each type floor(B*a_i/A); give the remaining integer points, one each, to the greatest exact fractional remainders, breaking ties by lexical canonical damage-type ID. Use exact rational arithmetic for this comparison. Zero-valued or ineligible components receive no share. No component becomes negative. Do not use a later resistance/immunity result to change the allocation.

Ordinary selected class flat reduction occurs before typed resistance/immunity. A selected Spinal Plating reduction occurs after typed resistance. Apply each type's actual immunity/resistance and its normal rounding exactly once at the authorized stage. Preserve typed residuals through actual prevention/transfer. Finish one grouped damage event per actual terminal recipient under Core/Null/lifecycle; no extra hit, Wound save, payment, resource trigger or replacement Null root is created by the component split. Other mixed-damage spells require their own express extension rather than inheriting this allocation by name.

KEEP Action, 60 feet, V/S/F, instantaneous 8d6 fire and blinding all enemies in the area. NEW center a 60-foot-radius sphere on the caster; this operationalizes the source range as its radial reach, with no remote center. Include every other creature in the sphere that the caster actually perceives and currently regards as an enemy, with caster-origin line of effect. The caster cannot selectively omit a perceived enemy after seeing saves. This is the caster's contemporaneous hostile selection, not a detector of secret allegiance, morality or hidden bodies. Allies, objects, buildings and worn/carried gear are not damage targets of this hostile-creature-only profile.

Each recipient makes one CON save. Roll 8d6 fire once. NEW success takes half, rounded down, and no blindness. Failure takes full damage and, after that packet, attempts Blinded on the same still-eligible creature if it could actually visually perceive this flash at the original snapshot. Sightless anatomy, an already fully opaque eye covering or an actual optical barrier prevents that visual rider, not fire damage. A real defense against Blinded prevents only that condition. Fire immunity can reduce damage to zero without preventing visually delivered blindness. Seeing the flash through ordinary glass does not defeat a glass barrier that independently blocks the spell's line of effect; both predicates must hold. A creature cannot claim a retroactive eye closure after learning its save; any genuine previously established closed-eye state carries its ordinary sight restrictions.

NEW this source-owned Blinded record lasts until the end of the target's next future regular turn, excluding a turn already underway when applied; outside initiative it lasts six elapsed seconds. It is nonconcentration and can end through actual condition cure, eligible removal/dispelling, death or its deadline. Suppression suspends the rider without resetting its clock. Ordinary Blinded consequences apply; unaffected nonvisual senses keep their actual scope. No whole-Action denial allowance is spent solely by Blinded. The flash grants no continuing light source, sunlight trait, Deafened, Stunned, Prone, ignition, blast movement or environmental fire. Subsequent entrants receive nothing.

## Chain Lightning — chain-lightning

| Actual class | School | Rank | Minimum class level | Base RP |
|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Crimson Agent | Evocation | 6 | 19 | 13 |
| Mage | Evocation | 6 | 11 | 13 |
| Wizard | Evocation | 6 | 11 | 13 |

### completeText

KEEP Action, 60 feet, V/S/F, instantaneous 10d8 lightning to one primary and arcs to three secondary targets. NEW choose one primary perceived creature or independently legal exposed object within 60 feet and caster line of effect, then up to three distinct secondary eligible creatures/objects that the caster actually perceives. Each secondary must satisfy the explicit same-point-pair primary-volume30-foot range and line-of-effect test below at the original pre-damage snapshot. A secondary need not be within 60 feet of the caster, but perception must be genuine at its actual distance. No one identity can occupy two selections, including a large body's overlapping spaces; the primary cannot receive a returning arc. Exclude hostile worn/carried gear unless an actual separate source permits targeting it. Independently targetable objects need real durability/material defenses; the spell does not invent a vulnerable HP track for a wall of force, artifact or undefined world feature.

For a direct cast declare all selected identities before commitment. For a legally readied casting, select and recheck those identities at release under the accepted Ready rule; spell/mode and all preparation costs were already committed. After the interrupt window, recheck the primary and all selected secondary predicates using the then-current positions and fix that one pre-damage snapshot. If the primary has become illegal, the spell has no anchor and gives no damage or arcs; costs remain spent. An unavailable secondary is simply omitted, with no replacement after outcomes. NEW every included creature makes an independent DEX save for full 10d8 on failure or half on success. The missing secondary quantity is expressly completed as the same full 10d8 lightning as the primary, not assumed from an external ruleset. Roll the raw 10d8 once for the whole cast; ordinary target defenses and separate recipient packets apply. Legal objects take that amount without a creature save unless their actual object profile supplies one.

The arcs are established from the legal primary whose actual primary application is permitted at the snapshot, not purchased by damaging it. A successful primary save, lightning immunity, full prevention of HP damage or death from its own packet does not cancel already established arcs. There is no attack roll or miss result in this save-based proposal. Each secondary's save, immunity or death likewise has no effect on another. Total effect-blocking cover on one arc removes only that arc at the snapshot; destroying that cover later in the event cannot restore or add it. All four identities share the one cast and raw-roll identity but keep separate damage roots and actual source-trigger scope. No recursive secondary arcs, rebound hits, new targets in conductive water, blindness, stun, ignition or ongoing electrical field are added. Up to three means a legal cast can have zero, one, two or three available secondaries without manufacturing targets or forcing harm to an unchosen ally.

NEW AC6 CURRENT INTERFACE COMPLETION FOR CONFERENCE: At the successful post-interrupt snapshot the primary must be a legal target and an operative primary application must be permitted, before it can supply this spell's secondary emissions. An actual field preventing that primary application prevents the dependent arcs; mere damage immunity/defense/prevention, save outcome or subsequent primary death does not. The real primary body at the pre-damage snapshot is the expressly authorized origin for each secondary arc for geometric/Globe predicates. This changes no dealer: the original caster remains each recipient root's dealer under Null. Recheck each secondary's actual payload defenses independently without retargeting, recursive arcs or a second target scan. This local origin/primary-application distinction is NEW and requires review against the accepted ward interfaces; it is not already granted by random Chain Arc.

NEW DC25-CHAIN-LOCUS-01, CHAIN LIGHTNING ONLY. Use the primary's exact actual occupied body or eligible object's collision volume snapshotted after real cast interruptions. A secondary qualifies only if there exists one same pair of actual primary/secondary points joined by an unobstructed segment of at most30 Euclidean feet. The distance witness cannot come from a blocked near face while line of effect comes from a different out-of-range far face. The caster must still actually perceive every selected secondary; this test grants no remote perception from the primary. This is an explicit new extension for this spell, not inheritance of random Chain Arc's target selection, range metric, public dice, owner or recursion. The original primary volume is the authorized physical arc-source locus; it never becomes a new caster/dealer. Snapshot it before damage. Primary immunity, prevention of HP loss, or its later damage-caused death is not absence of the original operative application and does not move this locus. Conversely, if that primary application is itself prevented at the established permission checkpoint, there are no dependent arcs. Determine each secondary's own actual prevention/cover at its original selected endpoint; a shared RNG value gives neither a shared recipient root nor a new application through a blocker.

Origin-sensitive accepted fields use that expressly authorized real primary arc locus for dependent arcs and the actual generating rank of this spell. Ordinary rank6 Chain Lightning is ABOVE the accepted Globe0–5 ceiling; do not claim that its normal arc is blocked by Globe, or reduce its rank merely because an RP discount/waiver applied. No origin is invented at a desired secondary point, through a wall, or at the caster's display cursor. The chosen selected identities and snapshot are not rescanned after damage.

Action; point range 60 feet; V/S/F; instantaneous. KEEP 10d6 cold in a 60-foot radius and actual water freezing. NEW choose a perceived point within range with caster line of effect. Snapshot all creatures whose actual bodies intersect the true radius-60 sphere with center line of effect, including allies and the caster when qualified. Each makes CON against the actual casting-class spell DC: full shared 10d6 on failure, floor-half on success, then actual defenses. This is one instantaneous damage pulse, not recurring damage, a stored orb, Frozen, Paralysis or automatic equipment damage. Roll/dealer/Null/recipient lifecycle follow the shared current rules. Cold immunity of a body does not itself protect separate water.

WATER SNAPSHOT. The environmental operation takes only the finite original ordinary exposed liquid-water volume within that same actual sphere, no deeper than one vertical foot below its preexisting exposed surface, with both a real unobstructed exposure path to that surface and the environmental operation's actual line of effect. Local prevention of that environmental manifestation excludes its affected part before a phase change; a later dispel cannot undo an already completed real phase change. Sealed roofs, ice caps, beds and solids are not scanned through. No later flow is added. Exclude living tissue/bodily fluids, water incorporated in food or another mixture, sealed vessels, and hostile worn/carried containers. A genuine caster-held or voluntarily offered open vessel can contribute only its actual water. Preserve salt, contaminants, custody, original water-source lifetime, no-sale restrictions and spent hydration history. No standard density, purity, extra Supply or new ingredient is inferred.

CLEARANCE — NEW DC25-ICE-GEOMETRY-01, FREEZING SPHERE ONLY. Resolve the instantaneous creature damage and its mandatory life/body/cargo consequences before the environmental change, without rescanning for new recipients or adding new water. Subtract every then-extant body and independent object volume from the original candidate water. For each such body/object intersecting the selected surface layer, also subtract its vertical expressed horizontal collision silhouette through that selected layer up to the preexisting exposed surface. This creates only an UNFROZEN WATER COLUMN, not air, an exit, movement, breathing, a new squeeze, or a tunnel. Do not shrink the silhouette to trap a larger expressed body. Where an actual obstruction prevents that straight vertical clearance, conservatively leave unfrozen the whole original selected-water connected component touching that body/object; other disconnected candidate components may freeze. Connectivity is through original selected water, never through a wall or a closed vessel. This deliberately replaces the prior undefined 'shortest route' with a bounded conservative exclusion. It does not repair an already trapped/drowning state or prevent later ordinary cold damage, source expiry, death or anatomy changes. No external event is cancelled to maintain clearance.

MATERIAL GEOMETRY. Phase change preserves the remaining water's exact occupied volume and actual recorded mass, with no pressure-expansion attack. Record one root material lot and its exact frozen solid geometry. World +z is up. Give it an immutable section grid: 5-by-5-foot x/y squares on world coordinates that are integer multiples of five feet; separate vertically disconnected slabs in a square are separate components. Clip each component to the actual sphere, surface-depth band, exclusions and original water boundaries. The section grid is accounting only, not permission to replace a curved intersection with a rectangle. Shared faces have zero volume and belong to only one accounting component by fixed x/y/z order. A smaller open pool contributes its actual clipped shape. No new ice fills an excluded column or connects two separated solids. Floating/ground support needs actual supporting water/ground; no unsupported bridge or levitation is created.

BOUNDED DEFAULT PROFILE. An independently established applicable ice/material profile wins. Otherwise each original full 25-square-foot component at least six inches thick has AC12, 20 maximum/current structural HP when first formed, ordinary weapon susceptibility, poison/psychic immunity, fire vulnerability, and at most 500 lb of load while truly supported. For every smaller or thinner original component, let f_HP=min(1, actual horizontal plan area/25 square feet, actual solid volume/12.5 cubic feet); initial maximum/current HP is floor(20*f_HP). This explicitly includes shallow puddle ice thinner than six inches rather than leaving its durability undefined. Its support budget is 500 times the actual >=6-inch supported horizontal area/25 square feet, capped at500lb; thin areas contribute zero support. A positive-mass component rounding to zero HP is fragile/broken material, not absent material, and supplies no creature support. Thin portions below six inches supply no default creature-support permission. Neither cutting nor relabeling a component grants a new full 20 HP/500 lb allocation.

SUPPORT AND BREAKAGE. The default supports a body's load only when its actual support contacts, or its full expressed ground footprint where no more specific contact profile exists, are continuously backed by qualifying >=6-inch supported intact ice. Distribute the actual load among overlapped original sections in proportion to that support-contact area; include carried weight and simultaneous existing loads. A section's current support budget never exceeds 500 times its currently qualifying horizontal area/25. Child pieces share that area budget, not independent 500 lb maxima. Fire thinning, cuts, holes, source removal and broken connectivity require an immediate support check. An overloaded component loses its intact support and becomes the same finite broken material; do not create crushing or an extra spell packet. Use actual Falling/swimming/body rules for an unsupported creature. The rule grants no automatic ground or water-breathing destination.

SPLITS AND HP. A physical cut must come from an actually legal source/tool operation with a declared real cutting surface; Freezing Sphere grants neither that operation nor free fabrication. If that operation genuinely partitions a component, child geometry is exactly the disjoint partition and their exact mass fractions equal volume fractions under this explicit spell-lot model. Allocate the parent's remaining maximum integer HP proportionally by exact rational child-volume shares, floors then largest remainders; ties use ascending child (min x,min y,min z,stable fragment ID). Allocate its remaining current HP proportionally to those assigned child maximum-HP capacities by the same rule, so no child exceeds maximum and both totals are conserved. Zero-max-HP positive-mass fragments stay real broken/fragile material. All children retain lot identity, contamination/custody restrictions and elapsed thermal exposure. Current HP is never reset by splitting. Voluntary recombination grants no restored HP, new section budget or unseen recipe.

FIRE AND PARTIAL MELT. Only an actual authorized fire/heat interaction with the material applies this profile; a creature-only fire spell does not acquire object targeting. For a positive-current-HP addressed component, resolve its actual defenses, including fire vulnerability, then let L be structural HP actually lost (capped at HP immediately before that fire). Melt fraction L/HP_before of its current intact volume AND mass into the same water. Fire overkill creates no extra water. NEW deterministic erosion: remove the topmost portion of that component by world-z horizontal clipping until precisely that volume has melted. Choose the highest cut level achieving the required volume when an empty-height interval gives ties. Keep the surviving solid at its original coordinates; record meltwater in the vacated geometry before ordinary environmental flow. This is spell-lot erosion bookkeeping, not global heat physics. Remaining current HP is HP_before-L; remaining maximum HP is max(remaining current HP,ceil(old maximum HP * retained-volume fraction)), never above the old maximum. Partition any disconnected survivors with the split allocation above. Re-evaluate support using actual remaining thickness/shape. At L=HP_before all addressed intact ice melts; no fragment of that same melted volume remains.

NONFIRE ZERO AND ALREADY-BROKEN PIECES. A nonfire structural loss to zero does not erase or melt mass: record a finite loose-fragment aggregate in the same original geometric envelope with structural HP0 and no intact wall/platform/support permission. It may be carried or separately partitioned only through actual item/carrying/tool rules. It receives no regenerated structural HP. NEW bounded heat completion: an independently legal positive fire-damage application to a specified already-broken zero-HP aggregate melts that addressed aggregate completely; not every other fragment in the lot. Ambient heat instead follows the remaining thermal clock. This supplies a defined finite result without an invented fresh smaller-piece HP profile. No loot, XP, source-cost refund or living damage-root trigger comes from relabeling broken material.

SLIP. Supported ice is difficult terrain. Make at most one DEX slip save against the original spell DC for this source lot in a creature's original regular-turn cycle, at the first voluntary entry or its regular START on the lot, never both. Failure makes it Prone under the actual condition; success grants no unrelated immunity. All child sections share that one spent-slip record. Extra turns, mode changes, leaving/reentering and splitting the lot do not reset it. At a published attached companion phase use that actual original phase/cycle; no new independent creature turn is granted. Without an original schedule, use fixed six-elapsed-second passive hazard intervals anchored to the first exposure, with no Action/Reaction/recharge refresh. Forced entry is not an immediate slip roll but a still-present body can face the next scheduled hazard check. If several independent such ice lots require the same first surface check, use the highest applicable source DC then earliest stable application-ID for one check, marking those simultaneous checks spent; this tie completion is Freezing-Sphere ice only. Existing Prone or actual Prone immunity does not cause extra fishing for rolls or a new disclosure permission.

THERMAL CLOCK AND CUSTODY. Absent a real applicable heat profile, each current material component fully melts after 3600 cumulative elapsed seconds in above-freezing conditions. Freezing conditions pause, not reset, that exposure. Splits inherit the parent's elapsed exposure exactly; subsequent distinct exposures can diverge per real fragment. Fire-melted water and remaining ice are disjoint portions of the same material ledger. Full thaw, earlier real source expiry or actual material removal ends only the material that source governs. An item name such as 'insulated' grants no new thermal rule. Real environmental heat may thaw earlier under its established profile. After the instantaneous phase change, suppression/dispel does not reverse physical ice, breakage or consumed water. Preserve actual contaminants, custody, capacity, use/sale restrictions and original source expiration throughout. No stored projectile, pure-water yield, rare stock or general refrigeration entitlement is created.

## Resolve a damage event

Resolve each genuinely separate hit or damage event once. Do not split one packet to multiply Wound saves or combine separate hits merely to cross a threshold. Feature-specific prevention that acts before damage or before reaching zero resolves at its printed timing first.

1. Resolve attack/save, damage and applicable defenses. The remaining nonnegative amount is **resolved damage**.
2. Record HP, Wounds and zero-HP state before the event. Remove temporary HP first. **Post-temp damage = resolved damage minus temporary HP absorbed.** Keep this amount even when it exceeds current HP; floor the resulting current HP at zero.
3. Check V6 Instant Death. If post-temp damage minus HP before the hit is at least maximum HP, the creature dies. For a creature already at zero, this is post-temp damage at least maximum HP. Stop: there is no Wound save, death-save failure or successful knockout from that event.
4. Otherwise, a living creature makes one Wound save if it dropped from positive HP to zero, if **2 × post-temp damage is at least maximum HP**, or if the effect actually inflicts Maiming. Overlapping triggers still cause only one save. The post-temp threshold and one-save rule are new provisional interpretations. Maiming is an independent trigger even when temporary HP absorbs all ordinary damage; immunity to Maiming itself still applies.
5. Make a Constitution save against **max(10, floor(post-temp damage / 2))**. Use pre-event Wounds for the -1-per-Wound penalty. Maiming with zero post-temp damage uses DC 10. A Wound gained now cannot penalize the roll that created it.
6. A success adds no Wound. A failure that began with zero, one or two Wounds adds exactly one; failure by 5 or more also gives that Wound one Injury. A failure that began with three Wounds kills the creature instead: no fourth Wound and no Injury roll. Stop processing later living-target consequences if dead.
7. If the creature was already at zero before this event and post-temp damage is positive, end Stable. A creature using death saves adds one failure, or two for a critical hit; three failures kill it. An ordinary creature that does not use death saves dies instead, without starting death counters. A hit from positive HP to zero does not also cause a same-packet death failure.
8. If this event newly reduces positive HP to zero, a valid melee knockout makes the surviving creature Stable. Otherwise a character/significant NPC using death saves begins dying; an ordinary monster dies. A previously Stable creature whose temporary HP absorbed all damage remains Stable unless an independent Maiming result killed it. The Director identifies significant NPCs consistently. Knockout cannot override Instant Death or a fatal Wound save.

Core's Maiming and Wound trigger rules remain subject to an actual feature's eligibility. The reference helper receives already legal events; it does not grant every attack Maiming or a damage-prevention exception.

## Zero HP and recovery

A living character at zero HP is Unconscious. Core's condition already includes Incapacitated, unawareness, dropping held items and falling Prone; an adjacent hit is critical as specified there. Healing does not silently remove other independent causes of Unconscious or Prone.

While dying, make one death save at the **start of each regular initiative turn**. Roll d20 without modifiers unless a feature specifically modifies death saves. Natural 20 restores 1 HP; natural 1 adds two failures. Other results of 10 or more add one success; 9 or less add one failure. Three successes make the creature Stable; three failures make it Dead. Extra turns and Actions add no extra death saves. Stable or Dead creatures do not roll them.

Regaining positive HP or becoming Stable resets both counters. Temporary HP does not raise normal HP, end Unconscious or reset counters. Fully absorbed damage does not end Stable or add a death failure in this provisional interpretation. Independent Maiming can still cause its Wound save.

**Stabilize:** an Action and DC 10 Wisdom (Medicine) check, or an Action using one healer's-kit use automatically, stabilizes a living dying creature at zero. It remains Unconscious and makes no death saves. Roll 1d4 once when it becomes Stable and record its absolute recovery time that many hours later. If still Stable then, it regains 1 HP. Damage reaching normal HP cancels that deadline and resumes dying with the appropriate failures. Rest and Stable recovery use the same elapsed clock, so four hours of recovery inside an eight-hour Watch do not cost twelve hours. A later rest does not separately replay the Stable healing event.

**Dead:** ordinary healing and stabilization do not restore life. Resurrection must satisfy its own willing-soul, body, time, cost and target rules. It does not automatically remove Wounds, Corruption, debts, legal death or claims upon the body. Corpses cannot supply repeated resource triggers.

###### role

## Movement and geometry

Track **spent movement cost**, not distance, for ordinary turn movement. Mode cap = `(1 + number of legal Dash actions this turn) × current Speed for that mode`; remaining = max(0, cap − spent cost). Switching modes never refunds cost. Recalculate cap on Speed changes, preserving spent cost. Stand from Prone costs floor(half current Speed) and requires nonzero Speed and enough budget. Dropping Prone costs no movement.

For each foot traveled, normal/difficult/severe terrain costs 1/2/3; use strongest terrain only. Add +1 for crawling and +1 for squeezing. Crawl in difficult =3:1; crawl+squeeze in difficult =4:1. A nonhostile occupied space counts as difficult terrain. Enter a hostile creature's space only with ≥two-size difference or an express feature; never willingly end in an occupied space. Squeeze through a space one size smaller, with own attack/DEX-save disadvantage and attacks-against advantage.

Walk10 through difficult spends20; switch to Fly30 leaves10. Stand with Speed30 spends15; switching to Fly30 leaves15. Two legal Dashes at Speed30 give cap90; prior expenditure still counts. Interpose/Press grant5 budget: they cannot enter a difficult 5-ft tile costing10. Their separate budgets never refill ordinary movement. Ready movement uses Speed at release; Withdraw uses half current Speed. All voluntary movement requires nonzero relevant Speed.

Forced movement does not use these ledgers or provoke ordinary opportunity attacks, cannot place creatures in solid matter, and stops at obstacles. Teleport does not traverse intervening terrain or spend Speed unless printed; it requires a legal destination. Speed0 alone does not block forced movement or teleport. Flight with Prone/Speed0/lost support causes Falling unless Hover or an explicit magical support exception applies; use the existing fixed Fall Marker procedure. Reaching support ends Falling.

NEW grid metric: each orthogonal or diagonal neighboring tile is 5 ft; use the same metric for ordinary ranges/areas. No diagonal cut through a corner when both adjacent orthogonal sides are completely blocked. Exact templates, 3D geometry, mounts, chases, jumping, climbing/swimming procedures and currents need further source review.

## Cover, sight, Hide and Search

Keep **visibility**, **known location**, and **line of effect** separate. Seeing through a barrier does not itself permit attacking through it. Best cover only: half +2 AC/DEX saves; three-quarters +5; total prevents direct targeting through that obstruction. Never add +2 and +5. For areas, evaluate line/cover from the area's origin to each target; total cover there excludes a creature unless the effect explicitly travels around/through the obstruction. The origin point itself must be legally targetable.

Within normal ranged range, no range penalty; beyond normal through long, disadvantage; beyond long, illegal. Ranged attacks also have disadvantage when within5ft of a non-Incapacitated hostile that can see the attacker, even if the attacker cannot see that hostile. All advantage/disadvantage follows the single cancellation rule.

NEW **Hide**: permitted only against observers unable to see you clearly or where a feature permits. Roll DEX Stealth once, record natural die and total. Against each observer's passive DC `10 + WIS Perception modifier including applicable proficiency`, use the Core four-degree task system. Meet DC succeeds before natural1/20 degree adjustment; Success/Critical Success establishes Hidden for that observer. No unsourced passive ±5 adjustment for advantage/disadvantage. Actual Search rolls use their normal modifiers.

Hidden means that observer cannot see you and does not know your location. Clear sight or learning your position ends Hidden for that observer. Moving between hiding places can retain Hidden only if no clear sight/revealing event occurs. A failed Hide cannot simply be rerolled unchanged; a materially new hiding position, concealment, distraction, sight-breaking event or express reroll feature is required. Hidden is not a generic immunity or a free stealth roll for every move.

**Search** Action: WIS Perception for sensory detection; INT Investigation for appropriate traces/deduction; Core Arcana/Engineering where relevant. Directly locating a Hidden creature defaults to Perception against its recorded final Stealth total as a static DC, using task degrees/natural shifts. Success reveals location only to the searcher and ends Hidden for that observer, without automatically granting sight. Communicate normally to inform others. Search can be repeated on later turns by paying the Action.

Attacks against unseen targets have disadvantage; unseen attackers have advantage, subject to the **Core Invisible exception**: once its position is known, Invisible's own attack modifiers end for that observer and cannot immediately return solely because it remains Invisible. Independent darkness, smoke, blindness or hiding can still supply modifiers.

Resolve an attack using pre-attack information; afterward reveal location to observers able to perceive its evidence. If an unseen target's location is unknown, do not directly choose it for a creature-target effect. A weapon/projectile can instead attack a suspected space: wrong space automatically misses; correct space uses unseen rules. A “creature you can see” requirement is never met by position knowledge alone. Area effects can target legal points under their own rules.

Real terrain/crawl/squeeze cost and forced movement

Movement, terrain, cover and perception

###### evidenceText

## Resolve a damage event

Resolve each genuinely separate hit or damage event once. Do not split one packet to multiply Wound saves or combine separate hits merely to cross a threshold. Feature-specific prevention that acts before damage or before reaching zero resolves at its printed timing first.

1. Resolve attack/save, damage and applicable defenses. The remaining nonnegative amount is **resolved damage**.
2. Record HP, Wounds and zero-HP state before the event. Remove temporary HP first. **Post-temp damage = resolved damage minus temporary HP absorbed.** Keep this amount even when it exceeds current HP; floor the resulting current HP at zero.
3. Check V6 Instant Death. If post-temp damage minus HP before the hit is at least maximum HP, the creature dies. For a creature already at zero, this is post-temp damage at least maximum HP. Stop: there is no Wound save, death-save failure or successful knockout from that event.
4. Otherwise, a living creature makes one Wound save if it dropped from positive HP to zero, if **2 × post-temp damage is at least maximum HP**, or if the effect actually inflicts Maiming. Overlapping triggers still cause only one save. The post-temp threshold and one-save rule are new provisional interpretations. Maiming is an independent trigger even when temporary HP absorbs all ordinary damage; immunity to Maiming itself still applies.
5. Make a Constitution save against **max(10, floor(post-temp damage / 2))**. Use pre-event Wounds for the -1-per-Wound penalty. Maiming with zero post-temp damage uses DC 10. A Wound gained now cannot penalize the roll that created it.
6. A success adds no Wound. A failure that began with zero, one or two Wounds adds exactly one; failure by 5 or more also gives that Wound one Injury. A failure that began with three Wounds kills the creature instead: no fourth Wound and no Injury roll. Stop processing later living-target consequences if dead.
7. If the creature was already at zero before this event and post-temp damage is positive, end Stable. A creature using death saves adds one failure, or two for a critical hit; three failures kill it. An ordinary creature that does not use death saves dies instead, without starting death counters. A hit from positive HP to zero does not also cause a same-packet death failure.
8. If this event newly reduces positive HP to zero, a valid melee knockout makes the surviving creature Stable. Otherwise a character/significant NPC using death saves begins dying; an ordinary monster dies. A previously Stable creature whose temporary HP absorbed all damage remains Stable unless an independent Maiming result killed it. The Director identifies significant NPCs consistently. Knockout cannot override Instant Death or a fatal Wound save.

Core's Maiming and Wound trigger rules remain subject to an actual feature's eligibility. The reference helper receives already legal events; it does not grant every attack Maiming or a damage-prevention exception.

## Zero HP and recovery

A living character at zero HP is Unconscious. Core's condition already includes Incapacitated, unawareness, dropping held items and falling Prone; an adjacent hit is critical as specified there. Healing does not silently remove other independent causes of Unconscious or Prone.

While dying, make one death save at the **start of each regular initiative turn**. Roll d20 without modifiers unless a feature specifically modifies death saves. Natural 20 restores 1 HP; natural 1 adds two failures. Other results of 10 or more add one success; 9 or less add one failure. Three successes make the creature Stable; three failures make it Dead. Extra turns and Actions add no extra death saves. Stable or Dead creatures do not roll them.

Regaining positive HP or becoming Stable resets both counters. Temporary HP does not raise normal HP, end Unconscious or reset counters. Fully absorbed damage does not end Stable or add a death failure in this provisional interpretation. Independent Maiming can still cause its Wound save.

**Stabilize:** an Action and DC 10 Wisdom (Medicine) check, or an Action using one healer's-kit use automatically, stabilizes a living dying creature at zero. It remains Unconscious and makes no death saves. Roll 1d4 once when it becomes Stable and record its absolute recovery time that many hours later. If still Stable then, it regains 1 HP. Damage reaching normal HP cancels that deadline and resumes dying with the appropriate failures. Rest and Stable recovery use the same elapsed clock, so four hours of recovery inside an eight-hour Watch do not cost twelve hours. A later rest does not separately replay the Stable healing event.

**Dead:** ordinary healing and stabilization do not restore life. Resurrection must satisfy its own willing-soul, body, time, cost and target rules. It does not automatically remove Wounds, Corruption, debts, legal death or claims upon the body. Corpses cannot supply repeated resource triggers.

One Wound on the stated failure; actual zero/recipient lifecycle

## Movement and geometry

Track **spent movement cost**, not distance, for ordinary turn movement. Mode cap = `(1 + number of legal Dash actions this turn) × current Speed for that mode`; remaining = max(0, cap − spent cost). Switching modes never refunds cost. Recalculate cap on Speed changes, preserving spent cost. Stand from Prone costs floor(half current Speed) and requires nonzero Speed and enough budget. Dropping Prone costs no movement.

For each foot traveled, normal/difficult/severe terrain costs 1/2/3; use strongest terrain only. Add +1 for crawling and +1 for squeezing. Crawl in difficult =3:1; crawl+squeeze in difficult =4:1. A nonhostile occupied space counts as difficult terrain. Enter a hostile creature's space only with ≥two-size difference or an express feature; never willingly end in an occupied space. Squeeze through a space one size smaller, with own attack/DEX-save disadvantage and attacks-against advantage.

Walk10 through difficult spends20; switch to Fly30 leaves10. Stand with Speed30 spends15; switching to Fly30 leaves15. Two legal Dashes at Speed30 give cap90; prior expenditure still counts. Dropping Prone costs no movement.

For each foot traveled, normal/difficult/severe terrain costs 1/2/3; use strongest terrain only. Add +1 for crawling and +1 for squeezing. Crawl in difficult =3:1; crawl+squeeze in difficult =4:1. A nonhostile occupied space counts as difficult terrain. Enter a hostile creature's space only with ≥two-size difference or an express feature; never willingly end in an occupied space. Squeeze through a space one size smaller, with own attack/DEX-save disadvantage and attacks-against advantage.

Walk10 through difficult spends20; switch to Fly30 leaves10. Stand with Speed30 spends15; switching to Fly30 leaves15. Two legal Dashes at Speed30 give cap90; prior expenditure still counts. Interpose/Press grant5 budget: they cannot enter a difficult 5-ft tile costing10. Their separate budgets never refill ordinary movement. Ready movement uses Speed at release; Withdraw uses half current Speed. All voluntary movement requires nonzero relevant Speed.

Forced movement does not use these ledgers or provoke ordinary opportunity attacks, cannot place creatures in solid matter, and stops at obstacles. Teleport does not traverse intervening terrain or spend Speed unless printed; it requires a legal destination. Speed0 alone does not block forced movement or teleport. Flight with Prone/Speed0/lost support causes Falling unless Hover or an explicit magical support exception applies; use the existing fixed Fall Marker procedure. Reaching support ends Falling.

NEW grid metric: each orthogonal or diagonal neighboring tile is 5 ft; use the same metric for ordinary ranges/areas. No diagonal cut through a corner when both adjacent orthogonal sides are completely blocked. Exact templates, 3D geometry, mounts, chases, jumping, climbing/swimming procedures and currents need further source review.

## Cover, sight, Hide and Search

Keep **visibility**, **known location**, and **line of effect** separate. Seeing through a barrier does not itself permit attacking through it. Best cover only: half +2 AC/DEX saves; three-quarters +5; total prevents direct targeting through that obstruction. Never add +2 and +5. For areas, evaluate line/cover from the area's origin to each target; total cover there excludes a creature unless the effect explicitly travels around/through the obstruction. The origin point itself must be legally targetable.

Within normal ranged range, no range penalty; beyond normal through long, disadvantage; beyond long, illegal. Ranged attacks also have disadvantage when within5ft of a non-Incapacitated hostile that can see the attacker, even if the attacker cannot see that hostile. All advantage/disadvantage follows the single cancellation rule.

NEW **Hide**: permitted only against observers unable to see you clearly or where a feature permits. Roll DEX Stealth once, record natural die and total. Against each observer's passive DC `10 + WIS Perception modifier including applicable proficiency`, use the Core four-degree task system. Meet DC succeeds before natural1/20 degree adjustment; Success/Critical Success establishes Hidden for that observer. No unsourced passive ±5 adjustment for advantage/disadvantage. Actual Search rolls use their normal modifiers.

Hidden means that observer cannot see you and does not know your location. Clear sight or learning your position ends Hidden for that observer. Moving between hiding places can retain Hidden only if no clear sight/revealing event occurs. A failed Hide cannot simply be rerolled unchanged; a materially new hiding position, concealment, distraction, sight-breaking event or express reroll feature is required. Hidden is not a generic immunity or a free stealth roll for every move.

**Search** Action: WIS Perception for sensory detection; INT Investigation for appropriate traces/deduction; Core Arcana/Engineering where relevant. Directly locating a Hidden creature defaults to Perception against its recorded final Stealth total as a static DC, using task degrees/natural shifts. Success reveals location only to the searcher and ends Hidden for that observer, without automatically granting sight. Communicate normally to inform others. Search can be repeated on later turns by paying the Action.

Attacks against unseen targets have disadvantage; unseen attackers have advantage, subject to the **Core Invisible exception**: once its position is known, Invisible's own attack modifiers end for that observer and cannot immediately return solely because it remains Invisible. Independent darkness, smoke, blindness or hiding can still supply modifiers.

Resolve an attack using pre-attack information; afterward reveal location to observers able to perceive its evidence. If an unseen target's location is unknown, do not directly choose it for a creature-target effect. A weapon/projectile can instead attack a suspected space: wrong space automatically misses; correct space uses unseen rules. A “creature you can see” requirement is never met by position knowledge alone. Area effects can target legal points under their own rules.

Real terrain/crawl/squeeze cost and forced movement

Movement, terrain, cover and perception

##### orderedAdjudication

At t0 after the Dash is complete, B spends Reaction/V-S-F/10RP on Mental Bomb. T INT natural2+8=10 fails16;4d6[3,4,5,3]=15 psychic, HP60->45, no Wound threshold. Debt records next future regular END8; Dash is not undone.

At t3 C legally begins Orb of Annihilation, Action/V-S-F/7RP:24->17. T elects its otherwise legal class Counterspell: real3RP affordability, Reaction entitlement, sight/range/LoE and trigger all pass.

Mental debt consumes T's selected normal Reaction and itself, but Counterspell never begins: no3RP debit, check, cast hook,2-round recharge or completed-Reaction event. T RP stays32. No recursive Mental Bomb trigger is produced by the denial.

The original Orb resolves:8d8 of4 each=32; T DEX natural15+2=17 meets15, takes16 force:45->29. No Wound threshold or zero event. At T START6 its ordinary Reaction refresh follows the existing rule, not the ended debt.

## Resolve a damage event

Resolve each genuinely separate hit or damage event once. Do not split one packet to multiply Wound saves or combine separate hits merely to cross a threshold. Feature-specific prevention that acts before damage or before reaching zero resolves at its printed timing first.

1. Resolve attack/save, damage and applicable defenses. The remaining nonnegative amount is **resolved damage**.
2. Record HP, Wounds and zero-HP state before the event. Remove temporary HP first. **Post-temp damage = resolved damage minus temporary HP absorbed.** Keep this amount even when it exceeds current HP; floor the resulting current HP at zero.
3. Check V6 Instant Death. If post-temp damage minus HP before the hit is at least maximum HP, the creature dies. For a creature already at zero, this is post-temp damage at least maximum HP. Stop: there is no Wound save, death-save failure or successful knockout from that event.
4. Otherwise, a living creature makes one Wound save if it dropped from positive HP to zero, if **2 × post-temp damage is at least maximum HP**, or if the effect actually inflicts Maiming. Overlapping triggers still cause only one save. The post-temp threshold and one-save rule are new provisional interpretations. Maiming is an independent trigger even when temporary HP absorbs all ordinary damage; immunity to Maiming itself still applies.
5. Make a Constitution save against **max(10, floor(post-temp damage / 2))**. Use pre-event Wounds for the -1-per-Wound penalty. Maiming with zero post-temp damage uses DC 10. A Wound gained now cannot penalize the roll that created it.
6. A success adds no Wound. A failure that began with zero, one or two Wounds adds exactly one; failure by 5 or more also gives that Wound one Injury. A failure that began with three Wounds kills the creature instead: no fourth Wound and no Injury roll. Stop processing later living-target consequences if dead.
7. If the creature was already at zero before this event and post-temp damage is positive, end Stable. A creature using death saves adds one failure, or two for a critical hit; three failures kill it. An ordinary creature that does not use death saves dies instead, without starting death counters. A hit from positive HP to zero does not also cause a same-packet death failure.
8. If this event newly reduces positive HP to zero, a valid melee knockout makes the surviving creature Stable. Otherwise a character/significant NPC using death saves begins dying; an ordinary monster dies. A previously Stable creature whose temporary HP absorbed all damage remains Stable unless an independent Maiming result killed it. The Director identifies significant NPCs consistently. Knockout cannot override Instant Death or a fatal Wound save.

Core's Maiming and Wound trigger rules remain subject to an actual feature's eligibility. The reference helper receives already legal events; it does not grant every attack Maiming or a damage-prevention exception.

## Zero HP and recovery

A living character at zero HP is Unconscious. Core's condition already includes Incapacitated, unawareness, dropping held items and falling Prone; an adjacent hit is critical as specified there. Healing does not silently remove other independent causes of Unconscious or Prone.

While dying, make one death save at the **start of each regular initiative turn**. Roll d20 without modifiers unless a feature specifically modifies death saves. Natural 20 restores 1 HP; natural 1 adds two failures. Other results of 10 or more add one success; 9 or less add one failure. Three successes make the creature Stable; three failures make it Dead. Extra turns and Actions add no extra death saves. Stable or Dead creatures do not roll them.

Regaining positive HP or becoming Stable resets both counters. Temporary HP does not raise normal HP, end Unconscious or reset counters. Fully absorbed damage does not end Stable or add a death failure in this provisional interpretation. Independent Maiming can still cause its Wound save.

**Stabilize:** an Action and DC 10 Wisdom (Medicine) check, or an Action using one healer's-kit use automatically, stabilizes a living dying creature at zero. It remains Unconscious and makes no death saves. Roll 1d4 once when it becomes Stable and record its absolute recovery time that many hours later. If still Stable then, it regains 1 HP. Damage reaching normal HP cancels that deadline and resumes dying with the appropriate failures. Rest and Stable recovery use the same elapsed clock, so four hours of recovery inside an eight-hour Watch do not cost twelve hours. A later rest does not separately replay the Stable healing event.

**Dead:** ordinary healing and stabilization do not restore life. Resurrection must satisfy its own willing-soul, body, time, cost and target rules. It does not automatically remove Wounds, Corruption, debts, legal death or claims upon the body. Corpses cannot supply repeated resource triggers.

## Time and event ordering — NEW

For a duration created in initiative, record the relevant original regular start/end marker. Existing effects keep that **virtual marker**, repeating every six seconds if the creature leaves or changes initiative. Reordering, rerolling, extra turns, death/removal or leaving/reentering initiative never restart an existing duration. New effects use the current schedule. Source death still ends concentration, a source-dependent aura/tether or an effect explicitly ending on death.

- A bare hostile target-bound **N rounds** ends at the Nth future regular target-end marker. An in-progress target turn's current end does not count.
- A bare self/source buff, ally-targeted buff or source-controlled aura **N rounds** ends at the Nth future source-start marker. Explicit target-turn, pulse or absolute timing overrides this default. Activation during source turn 1 for three rounds ends at source turn 4's start.
- **Next turn** means the next future regular turn of the named creature, not the current turn's still-upcoming end. An unqualified next turn refers to source/user under the existing shared rule. Explicit “this turn” can include the current turn.
- An outside-initiative bare N-round effect instead gets an absolute application-time + N×6 seconds deadline. Entering initiative preserves that deadline.
- Explicit periodic profiles retain their registered clocks and tick counts. Never create a second timer merely because a generic duration rule also exists.

The [exact GPT review](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_COMBAT_EXACT_FINDINGS.md) confirms ally-buff source clocks and excluding the current turn for explicit next-turn endpoints. The helper excludes a specific current-end marker only if that marker would otherwise be selected: application during a turn at time1 with end3 excludes3 and selects9; application at/after3 also selects9, without erroneously skipping again to15.

At a regular turn's **start**, resolve in this order:

1. Expire start-ending effects and apply immediate state changes from those expirations.
2. Refresh Reaction; tick regular-start recharge/non-action clocks.
3. Resolve already-due absolute state recovery if now legal, including a previously blocked Stable recovery deadline.
4. If still alive, dying and at 0 HP with death saves, make that turn's death save.
5. Resolve other start effects and periodic events in application order.
6. Begin voluntary turn decisions.

At **end**, resolve triggered effects, due damage, saves and maintenance before end-ending effects expire; then the slot finishes. No extra turn creates another regular marker. The caller must not reuse a resolved marker.

For simultaneous effects, earlier application timestamp precedes later. For simultaneous components of one source, its printed internal order controls; otherwise use stable application sequence, with source/mechanical ID as a final tie-break. Record that sequence when creating the effects. Beneficial/harmful labels do not change priority. Periodic family arbitration happens once per family/marker before its damage, retaining the family registry's consumed loser ticks.

Examples: dying at 0 rolls its death save before ordinary start-turn healing; a fatal death save is not undone by ordinary healing. Stable at 0 skips the save, then start-turn damage can break Stable. A natural 20 can restore 1 HP before a due Bleed drops it to 0 as a new drop event. If Decay expires while a Stable recovery deadline is already due, the old deadline resolves once without rerolling recovery time.

###### role

Original finite markers; no extra-turn refresh

###### descriptiveTopic

Regular and virtual clocks / START and END ordering
